> and August cache revision 237 (OpenRS2 cache 2504)

> `node --experimental-strip-types tools/diagnostics/trace-unresolved-npc-animations.ts`

>

> reviewed assignments into maintained runtime data and tests; retain this report only
> while it supports unresolved reviews. Never treat it as authoritative runtime data.

Generated 2026-08-27T11:46:09.380Z from OpenRS2 historical cache groups. August uses cache revision 237 (OpenRS2 2504).

Use `::npcreview <NPC ID>`. The review menu now loads each mapped historical batch automatically, testing newly added sequences first and modified legacy sequences afterward.

Within those two groups, `::npcreview` prioritizes matching gameval NPC families and IDs nearest to that NPC's cached movement sequences. This ordering is only a convenience heuristic; the complete release batch remains available and no candidate is discarded.

Where available, labels come from RuneLite's Jagex-generated [AnimationID gameval](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/runelite/runelite/master/runelite-api/src/main/java/net/runelite/api/gameval/AnimationID.java). Obvious NPC-family names are ranked ahead of VFX, projectiles, chatheads, and pet animations, but labels are still not server combat-role assignments.

These are release-window candidates, not cache-labelled combat roles. Every animation still needs visual review before it is assigned as melee, ranged, magic, defend, death, or special.
